Each row is one supervisor iteration. The log shows continuous progress through the graph. After each iteration, the supervisor:
1. Updated execution-state.yaml
2. Updated CURRENT_STATE.md and ACTIVE_NODE.md
3. Created durable records for any decisions/research/discoveries
4. Selected the next action
5. Continued

**Iteration 51 (2026-08-14)**: ML-001 RESEARCH COMPLETE — **RES-0014** recorded (`engineering/memory/records/research/RES-0014.md`) and registered in ALLOCATION.md (next RES-0015). Researcher subagent dispatch was cancelled, so the research was executed in-loop via live websearch (evidence-verified, sources cited with maturity levels). Node context: ML-001 is P3 EXPERIMENT (L3 ML for delivery-probability + gateway prediction), deps ROUTE-002 (RFC 6693 PRoPHET v2) + SIM-001 (discrete-event harness) both COMPLETE — the last un-gated software-only node before the graph stalls on BLK-0001/BLK-0005/LEGAL-001 gates (per GRAPH-0002, BLK-0002 expansion candidates remain). Key findings: (1) **RFC 6693 PRoPHET v2 is the Level-1 standard** and already implemented in ROUTE-002 — ML-001's scope is replacing only the DP computation, never the forwarding layer (GTMX+/SaW intact); (2) **ML framing = relay classification** (Dudukovich & Papachristou, NASA Glenn 2018 — XGBoost best classifier, PRoPHET > Epidemic); **ML-MaxProp** (arXiv:2508.20077, 2025) XGBoost over {contact frequency, buffer occupancy, hop count, message age, TTL} beats MaxProp/SaW in ONE (delivery ↑, latency ↓, overhead ↓), explicitly notes DRL/transformers are impractical on resource-constrained nodes; (3) **RL-PRoPHET** (Rezaei & Derakhshanfard, Wireless Networks 31:2909, 2025) learns the DP criterion itself — validates the ML-001 hypothesis; **DTRB MARL** (EAAI 26(10), 2013) canonical reference; (4) **gateway/egress prediction** — OppNet taxonomy (scheduled-DTN vs random-OppNet gateways per ScienceDirect survey), PodNet/mule Internet offloading; ferry/gateway prediction gains arise only under *periodic* contacts — a testable hypothesis for SIM-001's vehicle_relay scenario; (5) **learned routing**: GNN for UAV-DTN (ICCBN'24), decentralized graph-attention MARL for lunar DTN (arXiv:2510.20436), DRL under uncertain contact plans (INSA-Lyon 2025), RouteNet-TGNN (JCM 2026 — central-controller only, not on-node); (6) **constraints**: training offline/central, inference cheap on-node; SIM-001 can A/B L3 vs L2 same-seed; ML never in the critical delivery path (ARCHITECTURAL_PRINCIPLES #5/#6); (7) **eval design guidance**: periodic-vs-random scenario spectrum, held-out leakage guard, shadow-metrics only, ≥10 seeds with paired t-test/Wilcoxon per ML-MaxProp methodology. Graph: ML-001 RESEARCHING→DESIGNING; PROJECT_STATE researching 0 / designing 3 (CRYPTO-001, IDENT-001, ML-001). Next: ML-001 DESIGN — write `docs/implementation/ML_DESIGN.md` (experimental evaluation plan vs L2 baseline).
